14A-7-100">7-100&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Land and buildings in each of the following classified districts may be used for any of the following listed uses, but no land shall hereafter be used, and no building or structure shall hereafter be erected, altered or converted which is arranged or designated or used in a manner other than specified for the style="margin-left:0px;">C</div></td><td align="center" class="entry" startcol="13" style="border-color:#e0e0e0" valign="top"><div class="para" style="margin-left:0px;">C</div></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346775"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346775" title="§ 14A-7-200">7-200&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Classification of new and unlisted uses.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">In order to evaluate a proposal for a land use not identified by this Ordinance, a determination of the appropriate classification of any new or unlisted land use will occur as follows:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346776"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346776" title="§ 14A-7.200.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The City Secretary may refer the question concerning any new or unlisted use to the City Council requesting an interpretation of the appropriate zoning classification for such a use. The City Secretary shall submit to the Council a statement of facts listing the nature of the use and whether it involves dwelling activity, sales, processing, type of product, storage and amount and nature thereof, enclosed or open storage, anticipated employment, transportation requirements, the amount of noise, odor, fumes, dust, toxic material and vibration likely to be generated and the general requirements for public utilities such as water and sanitary sewer.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346777"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346777" title="§ 14A-7.200.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The City Council shall consider the nature and described performance of the proposed use, its compatibility with the uses permitted in the various districts, and determine the zoning district or districts within which such use should be permitted. The City Council shall, by ordinance, approve the referred zoning proposal, or make such determination concerning classification of the use as it determines appropriate.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346778"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346778" title="§ 14A-7-300">7-300&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Noise.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">At no point at the bounding line may a zoning district receive sound pressure levels that exceed the decibel levels specified in the groups designated in the following table:</div><div class="codeTable wideTable lightGrayHead tableV2 defaultBorders #e0e0e0" style="border-color:#e0e0e0"><table class="frame frameAll" style="border-color:#e0e0e0; font-size:80%" tabstyle="lightGrayHead" width="100%"><colgroup><col align="center" class="col1" width="33%"><col align="center" class="col2" width="33%"><col align="center" class="col3" width="34%"></colgroup><thead valign="bottom"><tr class=""><th align="center" class="entry borderRight borderBottom" startcol="1" style="color:#333;background-color:#d7d7d7;border-color:#e0e0e0" valign="bottom"><div class="para" style="margin-left:0px;">Residential Zoning District UE SF-1 SF-2 SF-3 MH MF-1 MF-2</div></th><th align="center" class="entry borderRight borderBottom" startcol="2" style="color:#333;background-color:#d7d7d7;border-color:#e0e0e0" valign="bottom"><div class="para" style="margin-left:0px;">Business Zoning District LR GB</div></th><th align="center" class="entry borderBottom" startcol="3" style="color:#333;background-color:#d7d7d7;border-color:#e0e0e0" valign="bottom"><div class="para" style="margin-left:0px;">Ag/Industrial Zoning District A LI HI</div></th></tr></thead><tbody valign="top"><tr class=""><td align="center" class="entry borderRight" startcol="1" style="border-color:#e0e0e0" valign="top"><div class="para" style="margin-left:0px;">Max dB Received 65</div></td><td align="center" class="entry borderRight" startcol="2" style="border-color:#e0e0e0" valign="top"><div class="para" style="margin-left:0px;">Max dB Received 70</div></td><td align="center" class="entry" startcol="3" style="border-color:#e0e0e0" valign="top"><div class="para" style="margin-left:0px;">Max dB Received 75</div></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="para">Decibel (dB) means a unit of measure of sound pressure, or noise level. An increase by ten decibels doubles the volume.</div><div class="para">Measurement of the noise shall be made with a decibel meter meeting the standards prescribed by the American National Standards Institute.</div><div class="para">The noise levels included in this Ordinance shall not be applicable to extraction operations or emergency vehicles.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346779"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346779" title="§ 14A-7-400">7-400&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Performance standards LI, HI (Light Industrial and Heavy Industrial) Districts.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">All uses in the LI (Light Industrial) and the HI (Heavy Industrial) Districts shall conform in operation, location and construction to the performance standards herein specified for odorous matter, toxic and noxious matter, glare, smoke, particulate matter and other air contaminants, fire and explosive or hazardous matter, vibration, open storage and glare.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346780"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346780" title="§ 14A-7-401">7-401&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Smoke and particulate matter.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">No operation or use in a LI District or HI District shall cause, create or allow the emission of air contaminants that violate State or Federal environmental laws. Open storage and open processing operations, including on-site transportation movements which are a source of wind or airborne dust or other particulate matter, are subject to the standards and regulations specified herein.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346781"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346781" title="§ 14A-7-402">7-402&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Odorous matter.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346782"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346782" title="§ 14A-7.402.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No use may be located or operated in a LI District which involves the emission of odorous matter from a source of operation where the odorous matter exceeds the odor threshold at the bounding property line or any point beyond the tract on which such use or operation is located.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346783"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346783" title="§ 14A-7.402.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No use may be located or operated in a HI District which involves the emission of odorous matter from a source of operation where the odorous matter exceeds a concentration at the bounding property line or any point the tract on which such use or operation is located which, when diluted with an equal volume of odor-free air, exceeds the odor threshold (2 odor units).</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346784"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346784" title="§ 14A-7.402.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The odor threshold as herein set forth is determined by observation by the City Secretary. In any case where uncertainty may arise or where the operator or owner of an odor-emitting use may disagree with the enforcing officer or where specific measurement of odor concentration is required, the method and procedures as specified by American Society for Testing Materials, A.S.T.M.D. 1391-57, Entitled “STANDARD METHOD FOR MEASUREMENT OF ODOR IN ATMOSPHERES,” will be used and a copy of A.S.T.M.D. 1391-57 is hereby incorporated by reference.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346785"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346785" title="§ 14A-7-403">7-403&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Fire and explosive hazard material.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346786"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346786" title="§ 14A-7.403.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No use involving the manufacture or storage of compounds or products which decompose by detonation is permitted in an LI District or an HI District except that chlorates, nitrates, perchlorates phosphorus and similar substances and compounds in small quantities for use by industry, school laboratories, druggists or wholesalers may be permitted when approved by the Fire Marshal of the City as not presenting a fire or explosion hazard.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346787"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346787" title="§ 14A-7.403.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The storage and use of all flammable liquids and materials such as pyroxylin plastics, nitrocellulose films, solvents and petroleum products is permitted only when such storage or use conforms to the standards and regulations of the National Fire Protection Association.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346788"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346788" title="§ 14A-7-404">7-404&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Toxic and noxious matter.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346789"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346789" title="§ 14A-7.404.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No operation or use permitted in an LI (Light Industrial) District may emit a concentration across the bounding property line of the tract on which such operation or use is located of toxic or noxious matter which exceeds the concentration (exposure) considered as the threshold limit for an industrial worker as such standards are set forth by the Texas State Department of Health in Threshold Limit Values Occupational Health Regulation No. 3, as such regulations exist or may later be amended.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346790"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346790" title="§ 14A-7.404.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No operation or use permitted in an HI (Heavy Industrial) District may emit a concentration across the bounding property line of the tract on which such operation or use is located violating State or Federal environmental laws. Open storage and open processing operations, including on-site transportation movements which are a source of wind- or air-borne dust or other particulate matter, are subject to the standards and regulations specified herein.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346791"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346791" title="§ 14A-7-405">7-405&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Vibration.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346792"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346792" title="§ 14A-7.405.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No operation or use in an LI District may at any time create earthborne vibration which, when measured at the bounding property line of the source of operation, exceed the limit of displacement set forth in the following table in the frequency ranges specified.</div><div class="codeTable lightGrayHead tableV2 defaultBorders #e0e0e0" style="border-color:#e0e0e0"><table class="frame frameAll" style="border-color:#e0e0e0; " class="litem_number" href="#39346794" title="§ 14A-7-406">7-406&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Glare.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">No use or operation in a LI or HI District may be located or conducted so as to produce intense glare or direct illumination across the bounding property line from a visible source of illumination nor may any such light be of such intensity as to create a nuisance or detract from the use and enjoyment of adjacent property.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346795"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346795" title="§ 14A-7-500">7-500&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Conditional use permit, purpose.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346796"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346796" title="§ 14A-7-501">7-501&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">This section provides the City Council the opportunity to deny or conditionally approve those uses for which conditional use permits are required. These uses generally have unusual nuisance characteristics or are of a public or semi-public character often essential or desirable for the general convenience and welfare of the community. Because, however, of the nature of the use or possible adverse impact on neighboring properties of the use, review, evaluation and exercise of planning judgment relative to the location and site plan of the proposed use are required.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346797"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346797" title="§ 14A-7-502">7-502&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The City Council, after a public hearing and proper notice to all parties affected may authorize the issuance of conditional use permits for the uses indicated by “C” in the Use Schedule 7-102 through 7-114.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346798"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346798" title="§ 14A-7-503">7-503&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">A building permit or certificate of occupancy will not be issued for any use proposed in a zoning district which requires a conditional use permit unless a conditional use permit has first been authorized in accordance with the provisions of this Ordinance.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346799"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346799" title="§ 14A-7-504">7-504&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Any person proposing to begin a use requiring a conditional use permit may file an application available in City Hall. The application shall be accompanied by a site plan. The site plan, along with the application, will become a part of the conditional use permit, if approved. The accompanying site plan shall provide the following information:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346800"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346800" title="§ 14A-7.504.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Data describing all the processes and activities involved with the proposed use;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346801"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346801" title="§ 14A-7.504.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Boundaries of the area covered by the site plan;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346802"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346802" title="§ 14A-7.504.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The location of each existing and proposed building and structure in the area covered by the site plan and the number of stories, height, roof line, gross floor area and location of building entrances and exits;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346803"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346803" title="§ 14A-7.504.4">4.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The location of existing drainageways and significant natural features;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346804"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346804" title="§ 14A-7.504.5">5.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Proposed landscaping and screening buffers;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346805"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346805" title="§ 14A-7.504.6">6.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Location and dimensions of all curb cuts, public and private streets, parking and loading areas[,] pedestrian walks, lighting facilities, and outside trash storage facilities;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346806"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346806" title="§ 14A-7.504.7">7.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The location, height, and type of each wall, fence, and all other types of screening; and</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346807"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346807" title="§ 14A-7.504.8">8.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The location, height, and size of all proposed signs.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346808"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346808" title="§ 14A-7-505">7-505&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The notification and public hearing process for conditional use permits or revocations thereof shall be the same as rezoning amendments.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346809"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346809" title="§ 14A-7-506">7-506&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">A conditional use permit shall be authorized only if all the following conditions have been found:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346810"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346810" title="§ 14A-7.506.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The conditional use permit will be compatible with and not injurious to the use and enjoyment of the property, nor significantly diminish or impair property values within the immediate vicinity;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346811"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346811" title="§ 14A-7.506.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The establishment of the conditional use will not impede the normal and orderly development and improvement of surrounding vacant property;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346812"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346812" title="§ 14A-7.506.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Adequate utilities, access roads, drainage, and other necessary support facilities have been or will be provided;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346813"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346813" title="§ 14A-7.506.4">4.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The design, location, and arrangement of all driveways and parking spaces provide for the safe and convenient movement of vehicular and pedestrian traffic without adversely affecting the general public or adjacent development;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346814"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346814" title="§ 14A-7.506.5">5.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Adequate nuisance prevention measures have been or will be taken to prevent or control offensive odors, fumes, dust, noise, and vibration;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346815"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346815" title="§ 14A-7.506.6">6.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Directional lighting will be provided so as not to disturb or adversely affect neighboring properties; and</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346816"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346816" title="§ 14A-7.506.7">7.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">There is sufficient landscaping and screening to insure harmony and compatibility with adjacent property.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346817"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346817" title="§ 14A-7-507">7-507&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Conditional use permits for on-premise sale of beer or mixed drinks.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346818"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346818" title="§ 14A-7.507.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">A conditional use permit to allow the on-premise sale of beer or mixed drinks, once approved, shall run with the land and be used in conjunction with the conditions approved or as modified, and is required for:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346819"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346819" title="§ 14A-7.507.1(a)">(a)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The sale of beer only, for on-premise consumption, where the gross revenue from the sale of beer for on-premise consumption is seventy-five percent (75%) or more of the total gross revenues for the establishment; and</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346820"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346820" title="§ 14A-7.507.1(b)">(b)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The sale of mixed drinks (includes beer), for on-premise consumption, where the gross revenue from the sale of mixed drinks for on-premise consumption is equal to or greater than seventy-five percent (75%) of the total gross revenue for the establishment.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346821"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346821" title="§ 14A-7.507.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">General conditions applicable to all conditional use permits allowing the on-premise consumption of beer or mixed drinks.</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346822"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346822" title="§ 14A-7.507.2(a)">(a)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The permittee must design and operate the establishment in such a manner that the proposed use or actual use of the premises shall not substantially increase traffic congestion or create overcrowding in the establishment or the immediately surrounding area.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346823"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346823" title="§ 14A-7.507.2(b)">(b)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The permittee must comply with applicable licensing and permit provisions of the Alcoholic Beverage Code within six (6) months from the date of the issuance of the conditional use permit by the City Council, such limitation in time being subject to review and possible extension by the City.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346824"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346824" title="§ 14A-7.507.2(c)">(c)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The permittee must demonstrate that the granting of the permit would not be detrimental to the public welfare of the citizens of the City.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346825"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346825" title="§ 14A-7.507.2(d)">(d)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The permittee must, at all times, provide an adequate number of employees for security purposes to adequately control the establishment premises to prevent incidents of drunkenness, disorderly conduct and raucous behavior. The permittee shall consult with the Chief of Police, who shall act in an advisory capacity to determine the number of qualified employees necessary to meet the permittee’s obligations hereunder.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346826"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346826" title="§ 14A-7.507.2(e)">(e)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The establishment must provide adequate parking spaces to accommodate its members and their guests. Provided, however, the number of parking spaces shall never be less than those required for similar uses in that zoning district where the establishment is located.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346827"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346827" title="§ 14A-7.507.2(f)">(f)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The permittee must operate the establishment in such a manner as to prevent excessive noise, dirt, litter and odors in the establishment or in the surrounding area and operate the establishment in such a manner as to minimize disturbance to surrounding property owners.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346828"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346828" title="§ 14A-7.507.2(g)">(g)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The City Council may deny or revoke a conditional use permit if it affirmatively determines that the issuance of the same is (a) incompatible with the surrounding uses of property, or (2) detrimental or offensive to the neighborhood or contrary to the health, safety, and general welfare of the City and its inhabitants.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346829"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346829" title="§ 14A-7.507.2(h)">(h)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">A conditional use permit issued under this section runs with the property and is not affected by a change in the owner or lessee of a permitted establishment.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346830"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346830" title="§ 14A-7.507.2(i)">(i)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">All conditional use permits issued under this section will be further conditioned that the same may be canceled, suspended or revoked in accordance with the revocation clause set forth in Section <span><span class="contentPreview"><a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-inherit MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways xref css-117jj87" href="https://ecode360.com/39346835#39346835" target="" rel="" style="cursor: pointer;">7-509</a></span></span>.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346831"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346831" title="§ 14A-7.507.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Conditions applicable to conditional use permits allowing either the on-premise sale of mixed drinks or beer, where the gross revenue from the on-premise sale of all alcoholic beverages is equal to or greater than 75% of the total gross revenue of the establishment.</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346832"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346832" title="§ 14A-7.507.3(a)">(a)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Conditional use permits for this use may be approved in the following zoning districts: Local Retail, General Business, Light Industrial and Heavy Industrial.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346833"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346833" title="§ 14A-7.507.3(b)">(b)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No conditional use permit may be granted if the establishment for which a permit is sought is within five hundred (500) feet of a church, public school or denominational school, public hospital, publicly owned park, or any residentially zoned or developed lot. All distances provided for in this section will be determined by the measurement to be made in a straight line from the perimeter of the premises to be permitted to the nearest property line of the said church, public or denominational school, hospital, publicly owned park, any residentially zoned district, residential lot, or any residential development. A location in a multi-storied building on other than the ground floor will be treated as though it were on the ground floor for purpose of this straight-line distance measurement between property lines.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346834"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346834" title="§ 14A-7-508">7-508&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">In authorizing a conditional use permit, the City Council may impose additional reasonable conditions necessary to protect the public interest and welfare of the community, including a time period for which a conditional use permit is valid. The City Council in considering and determining the additional conditions may impose such developmental standards and safeguards as conditions and locations indicate to be important to the welfare and protection of adjacent property from excessive noise, vibration, dust, dirt, smoke, fumes, gas, odor, explosion, glares, offensive view or other undesirable or hazardous conditions.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346835"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346835" title="§ 14A-7-509">7-509&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">A conditional use permit, once approved, shall run with the land and be used in conjunction with conditions approved, or as modified. A conditional use permit may be revoked or modified, after notice to the property owner and a hearing before the City Council, for and of the following reasons:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346836"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346836" title="§ 14A-7.509.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The conditional use permit was obtained or extended by fraud of [or] deception; or</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346837"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346837" title="§ 14A-7.509.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">That one or more of the conditions imposed by the permit has not been met or has been violated; or</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346838"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346838" title="§ 14A-7.509.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">That the conditional use permit previously authorized is determined to be detrimental to the public health, safety and/or welfare.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346839"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346839" title="§ 14A-7-510">7-510&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="litem_number" href="#39346840" title="§ 14A-7-511">7-511&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">A person making application for a conditional use permit shall pay a fee in an amount determined, and as from time to time amended, by resolution approved by the City Council, a copy of which shall be on file with the City Secretary and in accordance with Section <span><span class="contentPreview"><a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-inherit MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways xref css-117jj87" href="https://ecode360.com/39347299#39347299" target="" Any prospective property owners or lessees should review the official zoning map located in the City Secretary’s office before consummating any agreement based on the current zoning depicted on this map and/or the current use of this property.</div><div class="para">Such warning shall be placed in a conspicuous place on the map.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" standards.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">Two categories of Motor Vehicle Servicing/Repair are hereby established and they include Minor Vehicle Servicing and Major Vehicle Repair.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346843"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346843" title="§ 14A-7-521">7-521&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346857"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346857" title="§ 14A-7.521.2(a)">(a)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">vehicle servicing must be conducted completely within an enclosed building;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346858"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346858" kind;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346862"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346862" title="§ 14A-7.521.2(f)">(f)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">there shall be no parking of damaged motor vehicles, except on a temporary basis not to exceed 72 hours;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" in major repair of motor vehicles including any use listing below, as well as any use not listed as minor vehicle servicing:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346867"><div><a machinery/supplies; </div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346874"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346874" title="§ 14A-7.522.1(h)">(h)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">brake work or repair, other than minor maintenance in 7-521-1 (a–k) above.</div><div class="codeTable unnumberedParaPrevIndent paraTable " class="litem_number" href="#39346875" title="§ 14A-7.522.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Major vehicle repair development standards.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346876"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346876" title="§ 14A-7.522.2(a)">(a)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">this use shall be conducted within a building (although does not always have to be completely enclosed due to the nature of some of the uses);</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346877"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346877" title="§ 14A-7.522.2(b)">(b)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">this use shall observe a minimum separation of 25' to residentially developed or zoned property and 25' from public property such as a school or park;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346878"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346878" title="§ 14A-7.522.2(c)">(c)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">vehicle parts and wrecked vehicles may be stored outside of a building if screened from public view at any street, residentially developed or zoned property, or adjacent or opposite public property such as a school or park. Such storage shall never occupy more than 20% of the lot or tract; screening standards shall conform to provisions specified in Section 13-102-4;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346879"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346879" title="§ 14A-7.522.2(d)">(d)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">there is no vehicle size limit;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346880"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346880" title="§ 14A-7.522.2(e)">(e)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">signage shall conform to appropriate zoning district;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346881"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346881" title="§ 14A-7.522.2(f)">(f)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">customer parking shall be provided on the site at a ratio of one (1) space for each 200 square feet of gross building floor area, and shall conform to parking requirements in the zoning ordinance.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346882"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346882" title="§ 14A-7.522.2(g)">(g)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">site plan review by the City Secretary is required prior to initiating this use in order to ensure conformance with these conditions.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346883"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346883" title="§ 14A-7-530">7-530&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Standards for child care - small home-based facility.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346884"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346884" title="§ 14A-7.530.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Child care - small home-based facility:</div><div class="para">A private residence where the occupant provides custodial care and supervision for less than twenty-four (24) hours a day for a maximum six (6) children at any one time. The maximum of 6 children includes the family’s natural or adopted children under the age of 14. The residence must contain a minimum 150 square feet of floor area for each child. This use shall exclude a foster home and group home.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346885"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346885" title="§ 14A-7.530.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">This use is allowed by right in all the single-family residential zoning districts including AGRICULTURAL, URBAN ESTATE, SINGLE-FAMILY, MANUFACTURED HOME, and MULTIPLE-FAMILY districts, as well as in LR and GB districts.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346886"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346886" title="§ 14A-7.530.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">A safe loading and unloading location shall be provided. Off-street parking in addition to normally required parking for the residence is not required.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346887"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346887" title="§ 14A-7-531">7-531&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Child care center - intermediate facility.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346888"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346888" title="§ 14A-7.531.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Child care center - intermediate facility:</div><div class="para">A facility (including nonresidential structures) which provides custodial care and supervision for less than 24 hours a day for between 7 and 12 children, excluding a foster and group home. The facility must contain a minimum 150 square feet of floor area for each child.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346889"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346889" title="§ 14A-7.531.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">This use is allowed by conditional use permit in the residential districts including AGRICULTURAL, URBAN ESTATE, SINGLE-FAMILY, MANUFACTURED HOME, and MULTIPLE-FAMILY districts. An Intermediate facility is allowed by right in LR and GB and allowed in LI and HI only in conjunction with a business where this service is provided for employees.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346890"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346890" title="§ 14A-7.531.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Child care - intermediate facility:</div><div class="para">An off-street location such as a circle drive or another suitable location shall be provided for loading and unloading. Maneuvering room must be provided on the tract for parking and loading so as to preclude the necessity of backing out into public streets. A minimum of 2 off-street parking spaces shall be provided, with 1 additional parking space provided for each staff member in excess of 2. Loading, unloading, and parking areas shall be paved with either asphalt or concrete.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346891"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346891" title="§ 14A-7-532">7-532&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Child care - large facility.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346892"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346892" title="§ 14A-7.532.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Child care center - large facility:</div><div class="para">A facility where over 12 children receive custodial care and supervision for less than 24 hours a day, excluding foster and group homes.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346893"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346893" title="§ 14A-7.532.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">This use is prohibited in SINGLE-FAMILY, MANUFACTURED HOME, and MULTIPLE-FAMILY districts. A LARGE FACILITY is allowed by conditional use permit in A and UE districts. A LARGE FACILITY is allowed by right in LR and GB; and is allowed in LI and HI when provided in conjunction with a business providing this service to its employees.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346894"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346894" title="§ 14A-7.532.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The parking objective stated in 7-531-3 for an intermediate facility is identical for the large facility. In this circumstance, parking will be provided on the basis of 1 space per 400 square feet of gross floor area.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346895"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346895" title="§ 14A-7-533">7-533&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Site development standards.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">An applicant proposing a child care facility is required to prepare a site plan in accordance with the following minimum standards and the City Secretary will verify compliance and issue a letter of conformance.</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346896"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346896" title="§ 14A-7.533.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Location.</div><div class="para">Each facility shall be located in an area which is free from conditions dangerous to the physical and moral well-being of the children.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346897"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346897" title="§ 14A-7.533.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Construction codes.</div><div class="para">The building is subject to all other applicable laws, ordinances, and regulations of the city including, but not limited to, building, electrical, fire and health requirements, and enforcement by the City Secretary.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346898"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346898" title="§ 14A-7.533.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Play area.</div><div class="para">The play area shall be separated from the vehicle circulation and parking areas.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346899"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346899" title="§ 14A-7.533.4">4.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">TDPRS standards.</div><div class="para">The fenced outdoor play area, the indoor play area and all other pertinent standards as required shall be in conformance with Texas Department of Protective and Regulatory Services, and the applicant must document this conformity to the City Secretary.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346900"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346900" title="§ 14A-7-600">7-600&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Floodplain prefix to district designation.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">To provide for the appropriate use of land which has a history of inundation or is determined to be subject to flood hazard, and to promote the general welfare and provide protection from flooding, portions of certain districts are designated with a floodplain prefix “FP.” Areas designated on the zoning district map by a “FP” prefix shall be subject to the following provisions:</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346901"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346901" title="§ 14A-7-601">7-601&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Uses permitted.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">The permitted uses in that portion of any district having a floodplain “FP” prefix are limited to the following:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346902"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346902" title="§ 14A-7.601.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Agricultural activities including the ordinary cultivation or grazing of land and legal types of animal husbandry;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346903"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346903" title="§ 14A-7.601.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Off-street parking incidental to any adjacent main uses permitted in the district;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346904"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346904" title="§ 14A-7.601.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Electrical substation;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346905"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346905" title="§ 14A-7.601.4">4.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">All types of local utilities including those requiring conditional use permits when approved as provided in Section <span><span class="contentPreview"><a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-inherit MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways xref css-117jj87" href="https://ecode360.com/39346795#39346795" target="" rel="" style="cursor: pointer;">7-500</a></span></span>;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346906"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346906" title="§ 14A-7.601.5">5.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Parks, Community Centers, Playgrounds, Public Golf Courses;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346907"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346907" title="§ 14A-7.601.6">6.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Private commercial open area amusements such as golf courses, driving ranges, archery ranges and similar uses when approved by conditional use permit as provided in Section <span><span class="contentPreview"><a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-inherit MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways xref css-117jj87" href="https://ecode360.com/39346795#39346795" target="" rel="" style="cursor: pointer;">7-500</a></span></span>;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346908"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346908" title="§ 14A-7.601.7">7.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Private open space as part of a community unit development;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346909"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346909" title="§ 14A-7.601.8">8.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Heliport when approved by conditional use permit as provided in Section <span><span class="contentPreview"><a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-inherit MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways xref css-117jj87" href="https://ecode360.com/39346795#39346795" target="" rel="" style="cursor: pointer;">7-500</a></span></span>.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346910"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346910" title="§ 14A-7-602">7-602&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No building or structure may be erected in that portion of any district designated with a floodplain “FP” prefix until, and unless, such building or structure has been approved by the City Engineer, who will ascertain that such building or structure is not subject to damage by flooding and would not constitute an encroachment hazard or obstacle to the movement of floodwaters and that such construction would not endanger the value and safety of other property or the public health and welfare.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346911"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346911" title="§ 14A-7-603">7-603&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Any dump, excavation, storage, filling or mining operation within that portion of a district having a floodplain “FP” prefix shall be approved in writing by the City Engineer before such operation is begun.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346912"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346912" title="§ 14A-7-604">7-604&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">An area may be removed from the floodplain “FP” prefix designation when by the provision of drainage works, grading, flood protection or specific drainage study, it is determined by the City Engineer that the flood hazard has been alleviated. Removal of the floodplain “FP” prefix shall be accomplished by resolution of the City Council after written notification from the City Engineer advising of the removal of the flood hazard.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346913"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346913" title="§ 14A-7-605">7-605&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The fact that land is, or is not, within a district having a floodplain “FP” prefix shall not be interpreted as assurance that such land or area is, or is not, subject to periodic local flooding.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346914"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346914" title="§ 14A-7-606">7-606&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Flood hazard prefix designation.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">The City Council may, upon final determination that a lot, tract or parcel of land has no established history of periodic flooding but is subject to flood hazard and has not been designated with a floodplain prefix “FP,” in lieu of the floodplain prefix “FP,” designate said lot, tract or parcel with a flood hazard prefix “FH.” Upon final approval of any zoning, rezoning or subdivision of a lot, tract or parcel of land designated with a flood hazard prefix “FH,” said lot, tract or parcel, determined to be subject to flood hazard, shall be identified with an “FH” on the final map or plat filed with the City Engineer, or in the records of the County Clerk of McLennan or Falls County, Texas (the county in which the land is situated). The “FH” designation on said map or plat shall constitute notice and warning to any person or entity thereafter purchasing, mortgaging, leasing, building upon or otherwise dealing with the lot, tract or parcel of land that same may be subject to flooding.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346915"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346915" title="§ 14A-7-607">7-607&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Removal of flood hazard prefix.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="para">An area may be removed from the flood hazard prefix “FH” designation in the same manner provided for the removal of the floodplain prefix “FP” designation, established in 7-604 of the zoning ordinance.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346916"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346916" title="§ 14A-7-608">7-608&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">The fact that land is, or is not, designated with a flood hazard prefix “FH” shall not be interpreted as assurance or representation that such land or area is, or is not, subject to periodic local flooding.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346917"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346917" title="§ 14A-7-700">7-700&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Outdoor shooting range.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346918"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346918" title="§ 14A-7.700.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">General conditions</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346919"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346919" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(a)">(a)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">An outdoor shooting range may be allowed by conditional use permit in Agricultural, Light Industrial, and Heavy Industrial zoning districts. The approval process shall consist of two phases as follows: Phase 1: General Site Plan approval, and Phase 2: Detailed Site Plan approval.</div><div class="para">Elements required in each phase are identified below, Both phases shall include a public hearing and property owner notification as required for a zone change, and City Council approval.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346920"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346920" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(b)">(b)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Only handguns (pistols and revolvers) shall be allowed. No shotguns, rifles, or automatic weapons are allowed.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346921"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346921" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(c)">(c)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No armor piercing or incendiary ammunition is allowed.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346922"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346922" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(d)">(d)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Ammunition larger than 0.460 caliber shall not be allowed.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346923"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346923" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(e)">(e)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">There is no minimum range site (total property) required. Range, range site, and uninhabited downrange safety area shall be under uniform control and ownership, so as to maintain its permanency.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346924"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346924" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(f)">(f)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Range area includes the firing line; target line, backstops, side berms and walls; and baffles and other safety barriers. No portion of a range area shall be nearer than 100 feet to any public right-of-way, or adjacent property line.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346925"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346925" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(g)">(g)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Firing of handguns shall be prohibited during the hours between sunset and sunrise. </div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346926"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346926" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(h)">(h)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Range shall be used only under the supervision of trained personnel.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346927"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346927" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(i)">(i)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Appropriate certifications must be obtained from Texas Department of Public Safety.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346928"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346928" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(j)">(j)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Operators shall be covered by adequate accident and liability insurance in an amount of not less than one million dollars ($1,000,000) per single occurrence and two million dollars ($2,000,000) aggregate per year (combined single-limit for personal injury, death or property damage).</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346929"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346929" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(k)">(k)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Conditional use permit may be revoked at any time, following public hearing, as provided by Zoning Ordinance, Section <span><span class="contentPreview"><a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-inherit MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways xref css-117jj87" href="https://ecode360.com/39346835#39346835" target="" rel="" style="cursor: pointer;">7-509</a></span></span>.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346930"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346930" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(l)">(l)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No building permit related to the development of the shooting range may be issued until both Phases 1 and 2 have been approved.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346931"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346931" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(m)">(m)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Property owner notification shall include owners with property located in the ETJ. </div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346932"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346932" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(n)">(n)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Range shall conform in all respects to TCEQ standards and regulations.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346933"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346933" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(o)">(o)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Range shall be accessible by persons with disabilities and provide parking and restroom facilities as required by law.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346934"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346934" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(p)">(p)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Range operator shall be responsible for proper disposition of spent casings and projectiles.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346935"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346935" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(q)">(q)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Range operator shall be responsible for ensuring no alcoholic beverages are present on the entire range site.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346936"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346936" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(r)">(r)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Violation of any Federal, State, or local law shall be grounds for revoking this conditional use permit.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346937"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346937" title="§ 14A-7.700.1(s)">(s)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">No portion of range shall be located in a floodway.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346938"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346938" title="§ 14A-7.700.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">General site plan approval: Phase I</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346939"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346939" title="§ 14A-7.700.2(a)">(a)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Application form, application fee, and general site plan required. </div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346940"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346940" title="§ 14A-7.700.2(b)">(b)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">General Site Plan Elements:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346941"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346941" title="§ 14A-7.700.2(b)(1)">(1)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Identify and provide dimensions of range site and range area.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346942"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346942" title="§ 14A-7.700.2(b)(2)">(2)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Provide distances from range area to adjacent properties and roads.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346943"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346943" title="§ 14A-7.700.2(b)(3)">(3)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Identify all structures within 1000 feet of range site and provide distances. </div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346944"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346944" title="§ 14A-7.700.2(b)(4)">(4)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Show all existing utilities on site and within 150 feet of range site.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346945"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346945" title="§ 14A-7.700.2(b)(5)">(5)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Show all drainage features/contours.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346946"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346946" title="§ 14A-7.700.2(c)">(c)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Approval process requires a public hearing and proper notice to all parties affected, as required for a zoning change, and approval by the City Council.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346947"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346947" title="§ 14A-7.700.2(d)">(d)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Phase 2 must be completed prior to any construction or range development.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346948"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346948" title="§ 14A-7.700.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Detailed site plan approval: Phase 2</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346949"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346949" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(a)">(a)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Application form, application fee, and detailed site plan required.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346950"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346950" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(b)">(b)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Environmental assessment, sound study, and construction plans required, and must be prepared and sealed by a certified engineer licensed to practice in the state of Texas.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346951"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346951" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(c)">(c)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Application shall include water features/drainage plan, soils report, and sound study. </div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346952"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346952" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(d)">(d)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Detailed Site Plan Elements:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346953"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346953" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(d)(1)">(1)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Incorporate results of environmental assessment and sound study, including noise abatement elements.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346954"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346954" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(d)(2)">(2)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Site plan must demonstrate design will contain projectiles within berm/enclosure, within the range site.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346955"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346955" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(d)(3)">(3)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">If berms/baffles consist of tires, tires must be covered with earthen material. </div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346956"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346956" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(d)(4)">(4)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Range site and downrange safety area shall be surrounded by a fence of suitable height and material to exclude unauthorized entry by persons or livestock. Warning signs shall be provided along the perimeter of the fence. These signs shall be placed at the applicant’s expense and shall specify a firing range is present, operational during daylight hours. Firing of handguns shall be prohibited during the hours between sunset and sunrise.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346957"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346957" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(d)(5)">(5)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Paved parking, landscaping, and all other requirements of the Zoning Ordinance/Subdivision Ordinance shall be satisfied.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346958"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346958" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(e)">(e)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Construction plans shall be approved by the City Engineer for design. </div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346959"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346959" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(f)">(f)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_number" href="#39346960" title="§ 14A-7.700.3(g)">(g)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Approval process requires a public hearing and proper notice to all parties affected as required for a zoning change, and approval by the City Council[.]</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346961"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346961" title="§ 14A-7-800">7-800&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="title">Conditional use permits for modular and industrialized housing.</div><div class="para"></div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346962"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346962" title="§ 14A-7.800.1">1.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Modular and industrialized housing may be allowed by conditional use permit in Agricultural, Urban Estate, Single-Family Dwelling District-1, Single-Family Dwelling District-2, Single-Family Dwelling District-3, Multiple-Family Dwelling District-1, and Multiple-Family Dwelling, District-2 zoning districts. Modular and industrialized housing is allowed in a Manufactured Home District without the necessity of a conditional use permit. All modular or industrialized housing must comply with the requirements of this subsection <span><span class="contentPreview"><a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-inherit MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways xref css-117jj87" href="https://ecode360.com/39346961#39346961" target="" rel="" style="cursor: pointer;">7-800</a></span></span>, all other applicable provisions of this Ordinance, and all other applicable ordinances and regulations of the City.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346963"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346963" title="§ 14A-7.800.2">2.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Single-family or duplex modular and industrialized housing must obtain all local permits and licenses that are applicable to other single-family or duplex dwellings. For purposes of this Ordinance, single-family or duplex modular and industrialized housing is real property.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346964"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346964" title="§ 14A-7.800.3">3.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Any modular or industrialized housing shall:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346965"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346965" title="§ 14A-7.800.3(a)">(a)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">have a value equal to or greater than the median taxable value for each single-family dwelling located within five hundred (500) feet of the lot on which the modular or industrialized housing is proposed to be located, as determined by the most recent certified tax appraisal roll for the County;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346966"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346966" title="§ 14A-7.800.3(b)">(b)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">have exterior siding, roofing, roof pitch, foundation fascia, and fenestration compatible with the single-family dwellings located within five hundred (500) feet of the lot on which the modular or industrialized housing is proposed to be located;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346967"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346967" title="§ 14A-7.800.3(c)">(c)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">comply with City aesthetic standards, building setbacks, side and rear yard offsets, subdivision control, architectural landscaping, square footage, and other site requirements applicable to single-family dwellings; and</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346968"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346968" title="§ 14A-7.800.3(d)">(d)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">be securely fixed to a permanent foundation.</div><div class="codeTable unnumberedParaPrevIndent paraTable " style="border-color:"><table class="frame frameNone" tabstyle="unnumberedParaPrevIndent"><colgroup><col align="justify" class="col1" width="100%"></colgroup><tbody valign="top"><tr class=""><td align="justify" class="entry" startcol="1" valign="top"><div class="para" style="margin-left:0px;">For purposes of this subsection 7-800(3)(c) [7-800(3)], “value” means the taxable value of the industrialized housing and lot after installation of the housing.</div></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346969"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346969" title="§ 14A-7.800.4">4.&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">Any owner or authorized agent who intends to construct, erect, install, or move any modular or industrialized housing into the City shall first make application to the City and obtain the required permits, including a conditional use permit, if applicable. In addition to any other information otherwise required for said permits, the application for a conditional use permit or other required permit (if a conditional use permit is not required) shall:</div><div class="level"><div class="litem" id="39346970"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346970" title="§ 14A-7.800.4(a)">(a)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">identify each single-family dwelling located within five hundred (500) feet of the lot on which the modular or industrialized housing is to be located, and show the taxable value for each such dwelling as determined by the most recent certified tax appraisal roll for the county;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346971"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346971" title="§ 14A-7.800.4(b)">(b)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">describe the exterior siding, roofing, roof pitch, foundation fascia, and fenestration for each single-family dwelling located within five hundred (500) feet of the lot on which the modular or industrialized housing is to be located;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346972"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346972" title="§ 14A-7.800.4(c)">(c)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">describe the permanent foundation and method of attachment proposed for the modular or industrialized housing;</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346973"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346973" title="§ 14A-7.800.4(d)">(d)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">state the taxable value of the modular or industrialized housing and the lot after installation of the modular or industrialized housing; and</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div><div class="litem" id="39346974"><div><a class="litem_number" href="#39346974" title="§ 14A-7.800.4(e)">(e)&nbsp;</a></div><div class="litem_content content"><div class="para">indicate the deed restrictions otherwise applicable to the real property on which the modular or industrialized housing is to be located.</div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="footnotes"></div></div></div></div><div class="history">(<span class="legref" data-ref=""><span class="loclaw">Ordinance 2003-01</span>, sec. 7, adopted <span class="hisdate">3/4/03</span></span>;
